#### Research / Analysis

- BuilderGate TerminalView/TerminalContainer의 restore pending, direct write, early ready 경로와 Orca bounded pre-handler buffer·snapshot/pending sequence reconciliation을 비교했다. Existing BuilderGate limits를 재사용하고 UI/default는 변경하지 않는다.

| Date | Change | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |

| 2026-09-17 | Remount grace lane 을 실행 가능한 계약으로 승격 | Issue #6 의 AC 는 HEAD 에서 대부분 이미 구현·체크되어 있었고 AC-6 의 FR-BGSTAB-017 AC-3 supersession 도 AC-7 본문과 supersedes trace link 로 이미 기록되어 있었다 — 그 둘은 다시 도출하지 않았다. 남아 있던 실제 구멍은 증명력이었다: remount grace lane 은 WebSocketContext.tsx 를 문자열로 읽는 regex 단언으로만 지켜졌고, 그 게이트는 snapshot 블록과 held-tail 루프를 맞바꾼 AC-3 위반에도 전부 통과했다. 로직을 terminalGraceBuffer 로 그대로 옮겨 관측된 호출 순서와 유효 cap 경계를 실제로 실행해 검증한다. regex 게이트는 지우지 않고 새 모듈을 가리키도록 옮긴 뒤 무엇을 증명하지 못하는지 주석으로 라벨링했다. 사전 존재 실패 3건(REL-BGSTAB-012 ledger, B2 source inventory 2건)은 baseline HEAD 에서 동일하게 재현된다. |
| 2026-09-17 | grace lane 스위트의 generation-reset 축 보강과 VE-5 수치 정정 | 독립 리뷰가 새 스위트의 공허한 축을 잡아냈다. 반복 remount 테스트가 두 개의 독립 state 객체를 만들어 flush 했는데 프로덕션 bufferGraceMessage 는 session 의 map 엔트리를 재사용하므로, 정작 중요한 new-generation in-place purge 경로가 실행되지 않았다. 같은 결로 snapshot 교체 시 stale ready token 무효화, authority proof 없는 restore-needed 의 fail-closed 거절, 3-key duplicate 판정, overflow 이후 재축적 금지, reconnect-required 이후 admission 중지가 전부 미검증이었다 — 각각을 제거하는 변형이 새 스위트와 regex 게이트 양쪽에서 green 이었다. 여섯 축에 실패 케이스를 추가해 여덟 변형이 전부 새 스위트에서만 red 가 되게 했다. VE-5 가 인용하던 '관련 5개 스위트 178/178' 은 스위트를 지목하지 않아 재현 불가였으므로, baseline b34fda9 에 같은 변형을 적용하고 frontend 단위 전체를 돌린 tests 1258 / pass 1255 / fail 3 으로 교체했다 — 더 강하고 재현 가능하다. hold prefix 를 이 lane 이 제거하지 않는다는 판정은 하류 visibleOutputRecovery 의 screenSeq 기반 pruning 을 확인해 유지하되, 그 hand-off 가 end-to-end 로 단언되지 않는다는 한계를 VE-5 에 명시했다. |
| 2026-09-17 | plain-grace 경로 검증과 AC-3/AC-7 의 seam 분할 | 재리뷰가 두 가지를 잡았다. 첫째, 모든 테스트가 restoreNeeded 로 시드해서 plain-grace 경로(restore 미결 아님)가 통째로 미검증이었다 — 그 경로에만 있는 ready generation fence 와 snapshot prefix 제거를 각각 무력화해도 스위트와 regex 게이트가 전부 green 이었다. 그 구멍은 동시에 서술 오류를 드러냈다: 이 lane 은 prefix 를 제거하지 않는다고 적었으나 실제로는 plain-grace 경로에서 제거하며, 유지하는 것은 restore 가 걸린 경로뿐이다. 둘째, new-generation reset 이 output/outputBytes 만 검증되고 outputOverflowReason 과 authorityProofMismatch 초기화가 미검증이었다 — 둘 중 하나라도 살아남으면 완전히 수렴한 다음 generation 이 영구히 input gate 를 열지 못하므로 bounded convergence 위반이다. 네 축과 status/cwd/session:exited, 그리고 비-barrier 프레임의 flush 순서를 추가해 계약을 20건으로 늘렸고 16개 변형이 전부 새 스위트에서만 red 가 된다. 아울러 AC-3 와 AC-7 을 seam 에서 분할한다: flushGraceBufferedSession 은 프로덕션 함수이고 SessionHandlers 는 제품이 등록하는 seam 이므로 dispatch 순서는 프로덕션 producer 위에서 증명되지만, onOutput 이 scheduler 에 enqueue 할 뿐이고 gate 해제는 TerminalView 의 releaseRestorePending 이 소유하므로 drain·gate 합성은 이 lane 이 증명하지 않았다. 같은 이유로 remount 는 buffer state seam 만 덮고 lifecycle seam 은 덮지 않는다. 남은 절반의 소유(releaseRestorePending/VE-2, wave2-terminal-restore.spec.ts)와 오늘 판별력 있게 돌릴 수 없는 이유(#81)를 함께 기록했다. |
| 2026-09-17 | dispatch payload 정체성 검증과 AC 체크 범위 명시 | 재리뷰가 dispatch 절반의 overclaim 을 잡았다. flush 가 chunk 를 올바른 순서로 내보내면서 fromJsonOutputMessage 에 식별 필드를 넘기지 않아도 스위트와 regex 게이트가 전부 green 이었다 — 그런데 screenSeq/chunkId/replayToken 은 하류의 prune·dedupe·generation fence 가 키로 쓰는 값이다. 순서만 단언하고 'dispatch 절반은 proxy 가 아니다' 라고 적은 것은 단언보다 강한 주장이었다. payload 정체성 단언을 추가해 주장과 증거를 맞췄고, 이것이 §4 hand-off 의 producer 측 게이트 역할도 한다. 아울러 snapshot head 의 authorityProofMismatch 가드, snapshot proof-mismatch 분기의 held tail 폐기, input:rejected case, subscribedInfo 의 ready 억제, reconnect 이후 restore-needed 무시가 모두 미검증이어서 추가했다(계약 26건). 변형 표는 pass/fail 수 대신 red 가 된 테스트 이름을 싣도록 바꿨다 — '스위트가 잡았다' 와 '그것을 잡으려고 쓴 테스트가 잡았다' 는 다른 주장이고, 후자만 커버리지를 보장한다. 21개 변형 전부에서 의도한 가드가 red 이며(21/21), 그 측정이 두 가지 오기를 드러냈다: A 는 2건이 아니라 5건을 red 로 만들고, J5 는 regex 스위트도 잡으므로 'regex 가 전부 놓친다' 는 거짓이다(정확히는 21개 중 20개). 마지막으로 이 lane 의 보고서 체크박스가 AC 상태가 아니라 이 lane 의 증거 범위를 표시한다는 것을 명시했다 — SRS 의 AC-1~AC-10 은 VE-2 증거 위에서 모두 [x] 이고 이 lane 은 그것을 바꾸지 않았다. |
| 2026-09-17 | VE-5 를 현재 증거로 동기화하고 테스트 내부의 공허한 단언을 제거 | 4차 리뷰가 세 가지를 잡았다. 첫째, invalid-proof 거절 테스트가 빈 state 에서 시작해 restoreNeeded === undefined 를 단언했는데 그것은 프로덕션이 무엇을 하든 참이었다 — 그 분기가 필드를 비우기를 그만둬도 통과한다. 채택된 generation 을 먼저 시드하도록 고쳤다. 2라운드에서 닫은 결함(두 개의 state 객체 대 하나)이 이번에는 테스트 내부에서 재발한 것이다. 둘째, restore 분기 ready fence 의 snapshotSeq 절반이 미검증이어서 replayToken 만 게이트되고 있었다. 셋째, VE-5 가 한 라운드 뒤쳐져 같은 블록의 Change Note 와 모순됐다 — VE-5 는 계약 20건/변형 16개라고 적는데 Change Note 는 26건/21개였고, VE-5 는 독자가 '이 증거가 무엇을 덮는가' 를 확인하는 행이므로 그 모순이 가장 해롭다. 아울러 순수 디버그 케이스인 screen-repair/screen-repair:rejected 를 input:rejected 와 같은 방식(디버그 스토어 관측)으로 덮었다. 계약 28건, 변형 24개이며 24개 전부에서 의도한 가드가 red 다. VE-5 에는 이 lane 이 VE-2 의 HTTPS 실행을 재도출하지도 반증하지도 않았다는 것과 VE-2 의 PASS_WITH_LIVE_BACKEND_VERSION_EXCEPTION 예외를 함께 적어, 그 seam 의 다음 소유자가 체크박스가 아니라 증거와 그 한계를 물려받게 했다. |
| 2026-09-17 | VE-5 의 개정 이력과 그 lag 의 원인 기록 | VE-5 는 이 lane 에서 제자리로 세 번 고쳐졌고 그 중간값은 남지 않았다. 새 evidence 행을 더하지 않은 근거는 '그 행이 기록하는 시점이 의미가 있는가' 이며, 중간값(계약 20건/16변형, 26건/21변형)은 닫히지 않은 리뷰 루프의 반복이지 누가 서 있을 수 있는 완료 상태가 아니었다. 다만 읽는 사람이 진행을 알아볼 수 있도록 여기에 남긴다. VE-5 의 최초 형태는 '11/11 PASS' 에 변형 두 개(순서 교환, chunk cap off-by-one)뿐이었다 — 그 상태에서 스위트가 잡는 것은 그 둘이 전부였고, 지금은 31개 변형에서 의도한 가드가 전부 red 다. 그 두 숫자의 차이가 다섯 번의 리뷰가 한 일이다. 또한 이 lag 자체를 하나의 원인으로 기록한다: 매 라운드 Change Note 는 그 라운드의 실측에서 쓰고 evidence 행은 기억에서 고쳤기 때문에, 행이 매번 정확히 한 라운드씩 뒤처졌다. 4차 리뷰가 잡은 '행 20건 vs 노트 26건' 과 최초 상태가 어느 노트에도 없던 것은 서로 다른 실수가 아니라 같은 lag 의 양 끝이다. 교훈은 '같은 사실이 두 곳에 살면 한 측정에서 둘 다 도출하라' 이며, 같은 커밋에서 함께 고치는 것만으로는 부족하다 — 두 번째는 기억에서 쓰이게 된다. |
| 2026-09-17 | 세 번째 공허한 픽스처 제거와 VE-5 overclaim 정정 | 5차 리뷰가 세 가지를 잡았다. 첫째, snapshot proof-mismatch 테스트가 첫 snapshot 에서 mismatch 를 일으켜 current.snapshot 이 이미 undefined 인 상태를 단언했다 — 그 분기의 snapshot 폐기를 지워도 28/28+60/60 green 이었다. 채택된 snapshot 을 먼저 시드하는 형제 테스트에 그 단언을 옮겨 붙였다. 이 스위트에서 공허한 픽스처가 나온 세 번째이며 셋 다 같은 형태다 — 설정된 적 없는 state 는 지워졌음을 증명할 수 없다. 둘째, VE-5 가 restore 분기 ready fence 의 replayToken 과 snapshotSeq 양쪽을 덮는다고 적었으나 replayToken 절반은 어느 테스트도 소유하지 않았다(plain-grace 의 다른 가드를 덮는 테스트가 있을 뿐이다). 전용 케이스를 추가해 주장을 참으로 만들었다. 셋째, session:error, snapshot 교체 내용, exit code 문자열, status/cwd last-wins, subscribedInfo payload 보존이 미검증이어서 덮었다. 계약 31건, 변형 31개, 의도한 가드 31/31. regex 스위트가 함께 잡는 변형이 하나에서 둘로 늘어 '29개 중 누락' 수치를 갱신했다. |
| 2026-09-17 | 표본에서 열거로 — 문장 삭제 전수 스윕과 terminal reset 커버리지 | 여섯 번의 리뷰가 매번 새 축을 찾아냈다는 것은 변형 목록이 아니라 표본 추출률이 속도를 정하고 있었다는 뜻이다. 그래서 표본을 늘리는 대신 공간을 열거했다 — 두 export 함수에서 삭제 가능한 단일 행 문장을 전부 뽑아 하나씩 지우고 매번 스위트를 돌리는 스윕이다. 판단이 개입하지 않으므로 '어느 테스트가 전이를 단언하는가' 를 내가 옳게 분류할 필요가 없고, 그 분류는 이 lane 에서 이미 세 번 틀렸다. 결과는 56개 중 19개 생존이었고 19개 전부가 reset 문이었다 — new-generation reset 은 두텁게 덮여 있었으나 snapshot proof-mismatch, invalid-proof, reconnect-required 세 terminal 분기와 duplicate 분기의 중첩 authority-proof 검사는 같은 형태의 대입을 4~7개씩 갖고도 대부분 무방비였다. 그중 중첩 authority-proof 검사는 같은 토큰이 다른 authority epoch 으로 재발행되는 handover 를 잡는 이 lane 의 유일한 지점인데 통째로 미검증이었다. 필드별 점 테스트는 드리프트하므로 완전히 채워진 generation 을 시드하고 각 분기의 post-state 전체를 단언하는 표 기반 테스트로 닫았다. 재실행하면 56개 중 2개만 남고 그 둘은 §10 에 기록된 프로덕션 dead store 이므로 커버리지 공백은 0이다. 스윕 자체의 한계를 세 절로 적었다 — 한 절로 적으면 실제보다 강하게 읽힌다. 특히 열거 정규식을 처음 썼을 때 .push 와 += 가 빠져 있었고 그것은 버퍼에 청크를 넣는 바로 그 두 문장이었다. 제외 목록을 서술하는 대신 출력해 보고서야 발견했으며, 이 lane 에서 완결성 주장의 결함을 기록에 들어가기 전에 잡은 첫 사례다. |
| 2026-09-17 | AST 열거 기반 전수 스윕과 선행 Change Note 의 완결성 주장 축소 | 이 날짜의 '표본에서 열거로' note 가 '커버리지 공백은 0이다' 라고 적었다. 그 문장을 축소한다 — 참이었지만 오도한다. 당시 열거는 정규식이었고 그것은 내가 기억하는 문장 모양에서 나왔으므로 130개 statement 중 56개만 봤다. 놓친 74개에 모듈의 if 가드 25개가 전부 들어 있었다. 산술을 검산한 독자도 옳다고 확인했을 문장이며, 그래서 더 위험하다. TypeScript AST 로 두 export 함수의 statement 를 열거하도록 바꾸고 다시 돌린 결과 1차 130개 중 17개가 살아남았고 그 안에 실제 결함 둘이 있었다 — 바이트 동일한 duplicate restore 가 new-generation reset 으로 흘러들어 채택된 snapshot 과 held tail 을 지우는 것(row F 와 반대 방향), 그리고 reconnect-required 이후 ready 가 latch 되는 것. 또 recordTerminalDebugEvent 10개가 무방비였는데 이것은 커버리지 공백이 아니라 요구사항 공백이다: AC-2 와 AC-7 이 observable reason 을 요구하고, 올바르게 무시된 프레임은 자기 debug event 말고 흔적이 없으므로 가드되지 않은 emit 은 관측 불가능한 silent drop 이다. 무시 경로별 named event 를 정확 집합으로 단언해 덮었다. 계약 48건, 최종 스윕 130개 중 3개 생존이며 셋은 서로 다른 두 종류의 유예다(178·180 은 extraction provenance 를 지키는 dead store, 292 는 마지막 case 의 break 로서 결함이 아닌 no-op). 이제 완결성 주장은 두 축을 모두 적는다 — 삭제 연산자에 대해서, 그리고 AST 가 뽑은 130개 열거에 대해서. 열거 축을 빠뜨리는 쪽이 더 위험하다: 연산자를 과소진술하면 누군가 조건 변형을 시도해 드러나지만 들여다보지 않은 문장은 아무 신호도 내지 않으며 침묵하는 57% 는 덮인 57% 와 똑같이 읽힌다. 마지막으로 두 지배 관계를 남긴다 — 조건이라 열거에 없는 중첩 authority-proof 가드가 그 아래 reset 6개를 지배하므로 그 분기에서는 손 리뷰가 더 강하고, 통과하는 테스트도 주장보다 약할 수 있어(includes 가 정상과 결함 양쪽에서 참이었다) 스윕의 수정 후 실행이 스윕을 만족시키려 쓴 테스트를 잡았다. |

### FR-BGSTAB-021 — Generation-safe terminal clipboard ownership

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | wave-2 |
| Status | implemented |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | buildergate, stability, wave-2, terminal, clipboard, copy, paste, exactly-once, github-issue-7, feasibility:high |
| Risk | medium |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | Pure coordinator tests inject clipboard success/failure and generation changes; browser E2E on HTTPS verifies selected/unselected Ctrl+C, tab/grid/context/native paste exactly-once, IME and bracketed multiline; frontend typecheck/lint validate integration. |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/BuilderGate/issues/7 |
| Related Docs | docs/research/2026-07-15.orca-terminal-stack-absorption-research-and-implementation-plan.ko.md |

#### Requirement

Terminal clipboard copy와 programmatic paste는 target generation을 캡처하는 coordinator를 사용하여 copy 성공 뒤 조건부 selection clear와 accepted programmatic paste exactly-once 전달을 보장해야 한다. Wave 2 native Ctrl+V/browser paste는 명시적인 xterm owner adapter 예외로 두며 selection 없는 Ctrl+C·native Ctrl+V·IME의 기존 terminal semantics를 보존해야 한다.

#### Rationale

현재 tab/grid context-menu paste는 pasteInput coordinator를 우회해 sendInput으로 직접 전송하고, Ctrl+C copy는 clipboard Promise 성공 전에 selection을 지운다. 이로 인해 bracketed paste·barrier·generation safety가 source마다 달라지고 실패한 복사에서 선택이 사라질 수 있다. Orca의 pane-targeted clipboard coordinator와 exactly-once input ownership 원칙을 browser API에 맞게 흡수한다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Terminal copy는 action 시작 시 target terminal/view generation과 exact selection snapshot을 캡처하고 clipboard write가 성공한 뒤에만, 동일 generation·동일 selection인 경우 selection을 clear한다. Write rejection, context change 또는 selection change에서는 현재 selection을 보존하고 failure를 traceable하게 남긴다.
- [ ] AC-2: 선택이 있는 terminal copy shortcut은 clipboard copy만 정확히 1회 수행한다. 선택이 없는 Ctrl+C는 clipboard handler가 intercept·preventDefault·sendInput하지 않고 xterm/PTY의 기존 SIGINT 경로를 보존한다.
- [x] AC-3: 일반 탭 context menu, Grid context menu, registered preset과 그 밖의 programmatic clipboard paste는 TerminalView의 하나의 paste coordinator를 사용하고 direct `sendInput()`을 우회하지 않는다. 한 live browser/session/view generation에서 transport acceptance 전 reconstructed payload의 duplicate delivery는 0이어야 한다.
- [x] AC-4: Wave 2의 native Ctrl+V/browser paste는 existing xterm v6 native paste owner와 Chrome/Windows capture double-paste guard를 1차 adapter 예외로 유지한다. Shortcut handler는 reserved chord를 차단하고 native xterm onData 결과는 공통 input barrier, generation guard와 redacted debug capture를 통과한다. Native paste owner를 Orca식 coordinator로 이전하는 작업은 Wave 4에서 별도 수행한다.
- [x] AC-5: Programmatic paste coordinator는 bracketed-paste mode와 multiline safety, transport barrier/outbox, input debug capture와 context-change rejection을 모든 programmatic source에서 동일하게 적용한다. Unsupported multiline 또는 closed/stale generation은 다른 전송 경로로 silent fallback하지 않는다.
- [x] AC-6: Async clipboard read가 끝나기 전에 active tab/pane/session/view generation이 바뀌면 읽은 텍스트를 새 target이나 old disposed target에 전달하지 않고 explicit context-changed 결과로 끝낸다. Clipboard payload 원문은 durable logs에 기록하지 않는다.
- [x] AC-7: Copy/paste 완료 후 focus 복구는 기존 interaction style을 유지하고 성공한 동일 target에만 적용한다. 이 Requirement는 context-menu 시각, label, layout, shortcut mapping, UI feedback 또는 clipboard size/default를 변경하지 않는다.
- [x] AC-8: Exactly-once 보장은 한 live browser/session/view generation의 adapter admission과 transport acceptance까지로 한정한다. Reconnect operation ID, server dedup/result replay, OSC52와 native owner 전환은 이 Requirement 범위가 아니다.
- [x] AC-9: TDD는 copy success/failure/selection change/generation change, selection 없는 Ctrl+C SIGINT, tab/grid context paste, registered preset, native Ctrl+V Chrome/Windows double-paste guard, programmatic bracketed multiline, IME와 dispose race를 포함한다. Accepted input은 transport message 개수 1이 아니라 chunk 재조립 payload의 loss/duplicate 0과 one admission outcome으로 검증한다.

| VE-7 | automated-test | frontend/tests/unit/terminalClipboardAdapterContract.test.ts | AC-1, AC-3, AC-5, AC-8 | All programmatic paste paths use the generation-aware coordinator; the dormant legacy pasteInput bypass is absent from terminal handles and runtime proxies. |
| VE-8 | automated-test | frontend/tests/unit/terminalClipboardCoordinator.test.ts | AC-1, AC-5, AC-6 | Eight added cases close contract clauses that no assertion discriminated: copySelection's entry and no-selection guards, pasteClipboard's entry guard, pasteText's null-target guard, the currency recheck standing between a captured-but-superseded target and admission, the clipboard-read-failed rejection, the accepted-paste observation, and activate() on a live coordinator. Load-bearing and specific: an AST sweep enumerated 61 statements and 11 boolean operands (no regex), survivors fell 27 to 7, and for each mutant the sole failing test is the one named for that clause. The stale-target case was found by review rather than by the sweep, because every differential fixture had defined isTargetCurrent as identity over captureTarget, so the two agreed by construction, no input could reach that guard, and it read as a no-op because the harness could not pose the question; a fixture with independent inputs shows deletion admits a paste into a superseded target. Its observation is asserted whole rather than projected, so substituting null for the target or zeroing payloadBytes is caught - both are argument substitutions the sweep operator cannot produce. All seven remaining survivors, none omitted (S43, S48, S56, S58, S60, S61, B1), are no-ops on two independent grounds: mechanical survival, and zero divergence across 17776 scenarios each - mid-flight sequences of length 0..3 over ten state changes including target-currency flip and recurrence, selection-currency flip and recurrence varied independently so the false-true combination is reachable, a null selection mid-flight, dispose, repeated dispose, activate and selection replacement, times sixteen harness variants covering a rejecting admitPaste, a rejecting writeClipboardText, a captureTarget returning a fresh object per call, an isTargetCurrent that consults its argument, and two concurrent in-flight operations. The earlier form of this sentence described independence the fixture did not implement: one control set both currencies false and its inverse restored only one, so the false-true state was unreachable and selection currency could never return to true. Each is also a member of a group the suite catches when the group is deleted whole. Operator limit: deletion only; condition weakening, boundary shifts and operand reordering are not covered. Enumeration limit: the coordinator module only, so clauses whose behaviour lives in TerminalView.tsx are outside this row. |
| VE-9 | automated-test | frontend/tests/unit/terminalClipboardSurfaceWiring.test.ts | AC-9 | Keeps this requirement's unit verifying surface connected. Both clipboard unit suites were green and ran nowhere: absent from the tsconfig.test.json files allowlist, so typecheck:tests never compiled them, and named by no npm script. The guard enumerates clipboard unit tests from the tests/unit directory rather than a fixed list and checks four things: membership in the allowlist; that test:unit:clipboard leads with the node test runner after any npx, cross-env or VAR= prefix and passes each file as its own argument; that the raw script contains no shell control character that could discard the runner exit code; and that the program still type-checks strictly. Every clause is measured non-vacuous, each reddening only itself. Removing a file from the allowlist, removing the guard's own filename from the script, adding an unwired clipboard test file which reddens both set clauses, rewriting the script body to echo the same paths, and setting strict false in tsconfig.test.json which leaves membership and exit code intact while the check stops checking. Two earlier forms of this row were themselves evadable and review demonstrated both: accepting a runner token at any position let an echo-prefixed command satisfy every clause while running nothing, reopening an evasion a previous round had closed, and scanning whitespace-separated tokens for shell operators missed an unspaced or-else-true, a semicolon form, and a newline-separated compound, each of which runs a genuinely failing suite and exits 0. Both are now closed and all five constructions reproduce as red. Legitimate wirings are accepted rather than false-red: leading dot-slash paths and a cross-env or npx prefix all pass. What this row does NOT establish: the guard runs only when something runs it, and nothing does. test:unit:clipboard is referenced only by its own definition, typecheck:tests is invoked by nothing, tsconfig.test.json is deliberately outside tsconfig.json references so tsc -b does not compile these files, and the sole workflow runs only build scripts and no tests at all. Deleting the script still reddens nothing; that gap is issue #84, and allowlist membership across the other unit tests is issue #82. Enumeration bound: tests/unit only. |
| VE-10 | https-e2e | frontend/tests/e2e/terminal-clipboard.spec.ts | AC-6 | Partial run, recorded as partial. Against an owned https://localhost:2222 listener started directly from server/dist/index.js with Playwright's webServer disabled, 2 of 6 Desktop Chrome cases passed: 'late clipboard read during IME target switch rejects the old target without input' and 'late Grid clipboard read rejects when another tile becomes active'. The other 4 failed their precondition, not their assertions, with 'fresh PowerShell prompt did not become visible'. Cause measured rather than inferred: those 4 create sessions with shell powershell, and normalizeShellForPlatform in server/src/utils/ptyPlatformPolicy.ts returns auto for a Windows-only shell whenever platform is not win32, so on this Linux host the session is bash and the PS prompt can never appear; the captured page snapshot shows a live bash prompt. This means AC-2, AC-3, AC-4 and AC-7 have no browser evidence from this run and are supported here only by unit-level evidence in VE-8 and by the earlier VE-3 run. The spec guards on browser project but not on host platform, so on a non-Windows host these four report as failures rather than skips. |
| VE-11 | automated-test | frontend/tests/unit/terminalClipboardAdapterContract.test.ts | AC-3, AC-5 | Supersedes the textual half of VE-7's claim; VE-7 is left in place because it records what was once offered as evidence. VE-7 said the legacy programmatic paste bypass is absent from terminal handles. As implemented that was two doesNotMatch assertions against source text for the token pasteInput, which occurs nowhere in the repository, so both were vacuously true and could not fail for any bypass not spelled with that exact identifier. Four successive forms of the replacement were each a proxy standing in for the property and each was defeated by review: a name pattern missed a member called insertText; identifier-only name resolution dropped a quoted member so the exact set compared equal over a subset; a substring delegation match accepted a body that called sendInput and then returned the coordinator result; and pinning the TerminalHandle interface missed a member added only to the useImperativeHandle object literal, on either component, because useImperativeHandle admits members the interface does not declare. The current form pins three surfaces: the interface, and both imperative-handle object literals, which are the objects callers actually receive. Each pinned name carries the reason it is not a paste entry point, and every member whose signature takes a lone string parameter must additionally be named in a separate list, so admitting a new payload sink costs a decision rather than a keystroke. Paste delegation must reduce to a single call whose callee is the coordinator, with the payload forwarded unmodified; block bodies are rejected, and a nullish-coalescing fallback may neither call anything outside a named inert set nor reference a delivery seam, because that fallback fires exactly when the primary target is missing. Measured against every construction review produced, each reddening only this test: a literal-only member on the view, a literal-only member on the container, a delivering fallback, a duplicated payload argument, an interface-only member, a quoted member name, and a block-body double delivery. Scope limit, stated as what it holds: no member exists outside the pinned set on the interface or on either literal, and the two paste members' initializers reduce to one coordinator call forwarding their parameters. It does not execute either component, so runtime behaviour, including selection-less Ctrl+C SIGINT ownership, remains outside every unit row on this requirement; issue #87 owns that. |
| VE-12 | https-e2e | frontend/tests/e2e/terminal-clipboard.spec.ts | AC-2 | Records an OPEN question against AC-2 and an attempt to settle it that failed, not a verification. Round-five review raised a code-path argument: the Ctrl+C gate is satisfied by a saved right-click selection whose ref is cleared only by mouse button 0, clearSelection, and terminal create or dispose, so neither dismissing the context menu nor typing clears it and a later Ctrl+C could be intercepted with no visible selection. The reviewer declined to certify what it could not execute and said a browser would settle it. This lane attempted that against an owned https://localhost:2222 listener and the attempt was INVALID: the probe reported a swallowed SIGINT three times, and a control added afterwards showed that typing between the Escape and the Ctrl+C produced zero input frames, so the terminal never held keyboard focus and focus rather than interception accounts for every zero. Reading the source afterwards makes the question harder rather than easier: ContextMenu restores focus on close through closeAndRestoreFocus, but the document-level Escape listener calls onClose directly without that restore, so the two Escape paths differ. A confirmation must therefore show keyboard focus and a non-empty saved reference COEXISTING, and must prove focus by observing input frames from typing rather than by clicking, because clicking is mouse button 0 and clears the very reference under test. AC-2 is unchecked for this reason: the question is open, owned by issue #88, and this row exists so that the open state is not mistaken for an unverified oversight. |

#### Research / Analysis

- BuilderGate App/Mosaic/TerminalView의 서로 다른 clipboard 경로와 read-only Orca terminal-pane keyboard/context-menu/paste coordinator를 비교했다. Electron IPC는 복사하지 않고 target identity, promise completion, paste ownership 원칙만 채택한다.

#### Implementation Notes

- -

#### Change Notes

| Date | Change | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-07-15 | Created | add-requirement |
| 2026-07-16 | Status -> in_progress | Wave 2 PH-004 begins with strict RED tests for a single target-aware clipboard coordinator; no production behavior changes precede the failing contract. |
| 2026-07-16 | Status -> implemented | PH-004 strict TDD, HTTPS strict/full E2E, typecheck/build, scoped lint와 mandatory review loop를 완료했다. Clipboard 원문을 telemetry에 남기지 않고 모든 AC를 자동 증거로 확인했다. |
| 2026-09-17 | Verifying surface reconnected and mutation-hardened; no production change | Issue #7's six acceptance criteria were already satisfied by production code at the time of this work: all programmatic paste routes through the coordinator, selection clears only after a successful clipboard write for the same generation and selection, failures reach the debug channel, and selection-less Ctrl+C falls through to the native SIGINT owner. The defect was in the verification: both clipboard unit suites compiled in no TypeScript program and were named by no script. They are now in the tsconfig.test.json allowlist and in a test:unit:clipboard script, with a self-including guard (VE-9) that fails if either connection is lost. An AST mutation sweep then found seven contract clauses that no assertion discriminated, now closed (VE-8). terminalClipboardCoordinator.ts is byte-identical to its prior state. Status remains implemented rather than verified: browser evidence is partial because four of six e2e cases require a PowerShell host (VE-10). The Rationale above is deliberately left describing the pre-implementation state, as authoring-time motivation rather than a current claim. |
| 2026-09-17 | VE-8 scope corrected and one further guard covered after independent review | Independent review falsified two claims in the first pass. VE-8 had listed AC-2, whose operative clause is that a selection-less Ctrl+C is not intercepted; that behaviour lives in TerminalView's keydown handler, which the coordinator suite never loads, so the row claimed coverage its artifact cannot observe and AC-2 has been removed from it. Review also showed that every differential fixture defined isTargetCurrent as identity over captureTarget, so the two agreed by construction and no input could reach the currency recheck guarding admission; it had been recorded as a no-op when it is load-bearing, and deleting it admits a paste into a superseded target. A test now covers it and survivors fell from nine to seven. A falsification script had additionally used a first-occurrence string replacement where three substitutions were intended, so one group mutant was under-applied; corrected and re-run, that group is caught. Records that rested on the pre-fix run have been re-derived from the post-fix run rather than restated. |
| 2026-09-17 | Round-two review retractions and the last coverage gap closed | Retracting an overclaim in the first note of this series: it said the guard fails if either connection is lost. That is not true of the connection that matters most. The guard runs only when something runs it, and nothing does - the script is referenced only by its own definition, typecheck:tests is invoked by nothing, and the sole workflow runs build scripts and no tests. VE-9 now states that limitation and issue #84 owns it. Two further claims were falsified and are corrected rather than restated. A differential no-op claim covered seven survivors while the fixture behind it had been given only three of them, which is the same shape as the fixture-agreement defect recorded in the previous note, one level up; all seven are now run through it across 1600 scenarios each. And the test asserting that terminal handles expose no legacy paste bypass was two absence-of-a-token assertions against source text for an identifier that occurs nowhere in the repository, so it was vacuously true and admitted a real bypass; it now enumerates the handle from the AST and is measured against that bypass. VE-11 records it and VE-7 is left in place as the record of what was once offered. |
| 2026-09-17 | Rationale superseded, not rewritten: both of its present-tense claims measured false | The Rationale states two defects in the present tense and both are false as of this requirement's implementation. Claim one: that tab and grid context-menu paste bypass the coordinator and send directly through sendInput. Measured false - App.tsx routes tab-context-menu and command-preset paste, and MosaicContainer.tsx routes grid-context-menu paste, through the TerminalView imperative handle into the clipboard coordinator, and an AST enumeration of the TerminalHandle interface shows the paste-capable member set is exactly pasteClipboard and pasteText, both delegating to that coordinator, with no sendInput paste path remaining. Claim two: that Ctrl+C copy clears the selection before the clipboard promise succeeds. Measured false - copySelection awaits writeClipboardText and clears only when the captured target is still current and the captured selection still matches, and both operands of that condition are individually discriminated by tests, one of them by the test named for it. The original text is deliberately left intact rather than edited, because it records the motivation at authoring time and rewriting it would remove the reason this requirement exists. This note supersedes it as a statement about the current system. |
| 2026-09-17 | Round-three review: five guards written the previous round were evadable | Eight findings, all reproduced before acceptance, and five of them defeat guards added in the previous round rather than anything older. The handle enumeration gated its exact-set check on member names matching paste, which verifies a naming convention and not the property: a member named insertText delivering through sendInput is a genuine programmatic paste bypass that the filter never sees. Identifier-only name resolution silently dropped a quoted member, so the exact set compared equal over a subset and VE-11's own worked example was defeated by one pair of quotes. The delegation check was a substring match, so a block body that calls sendInput and then returns the coordinator result passed while delivering twice, which is the exactly-once violation the row is cited for. The guard's runner check had been loosened in the previous round to stop false-reddening legitimate wirings, and that loosening reopened an evasion the same round had closed - an echo-prefixed command satisfied every clause while running nothing. Its shell-operator filter scanned whitespace-separated tokens, so an unspaced or-else-true, a semicolon form and a newline compound all ran a failing suite and exited 0. Two further items: the commit that removed a vacuous assertion added one, a regular expression run against a string literal that is constant and can never fail; and the differential fixture's comment claimed selection currency was varied independently of target currency when one control set both false and its inverse restored only one, leaving a state unreachable. All are fixed, each fix measured against the exact construction that motivated it, and the fixture now varies every axis its description claims across 17776 scenarios per mutant. Survivors remain seven after a fifth sweep. |
| 2026-09-17 | Round four: the pinned surface was the type, not the shipped object | Seven findings, all reproduced before acceptance, and the central one is that the previous round pinned the TerminalHandle interface while useImperativeHandle admits object-literal members the interface never declares. A bypass added to the view's literal alone, or to the container's literal alone, passed with every test green, and the container was never enumerated at all because the member walk ran only on the view. That is the fourth successive form of one defect: a name pattern, an identifier-only walk, a substring match and now a type declaration, each a proxy standing in for the property that no paste entry point exists outside the coordinator. The current form stops choosing proxies and pins all three shipped surfaces. Three narrower findings followed the same shape: unwrapping a nullish-coalescing expression to its left operand left the right operand unchecked although it executes precisely when the left is nullish, so a fallback could deliver; comparing only the callee let a duplicated payload argument through, which is the exactly-once class this requirement is cited for; and the raw-string shell scan reddened two legitimate wirings, a quoted test-name pattern and a path containing an ampersand, which invites a maintainer to weaken it. Each is fixed and measured against the exact construction that motivated it, and after loosening the shell scan the four evasions it exists to catch were re-run and remain red, because a previous round's fix for a false positive had reopened a true negative. The last finding was design rather than defect and is acted on: a bare list of member names makes the cheapest response to a future failure a copied name, so each pinned name now carries the reason it is not a paste entry point and any member taking a lone string parameter must additionally be named in a separate list. Survivors remain seven after a sixth sweep. Production code is unchanged throughout. |
| 2026-09-17 | AC-2 unchecked: an open code-path argument, and an invalid attempt to settle it | Round-five review raised an argument that the Ctrl+C gate can be satisfied by a saved right-click selection after every visible sign of that selection is gone, which would mean a selection-less Ctrl+C does not reach the native SIGINT path. The reviewer declined to certify what it could not execute. This lane ran a browser probe and reported a confirmed reproduction, three runs, control one SIGINT and subject zero. That report was wrong and is withdrawn: a control added afterwards showed the typing between the Escape and the Ctrl+C produced zero input frames, so the terminal never held keyboard focus and focus rather than interception explains every zero. The defect was in the instrument, not the product, and it is the first in this lane that failed toward a false positive rather than a false all-clear. AC-2 is therefore unchecked rather than left checked, because an open uncertified argument means the verification claim is not held; it is not evidence that the criterion is violated either. Issue #88 owns the question, with three things this lane learned recorded there: a confirmation must show keyboard focus and a non-empty saved reference coexisting; focus must be proven by observing input frames from typing rather than by clicking, since clicking is mouse button 0 and clears the reference under test; and the source has two Escape paths with different focus outcomes, because the menu's own close restores focus while the document-level listener does not. The probe and all of its run logs, including the invalid ones, are handed to that issue rather than committed here, so the next attempt does not rebuild the same confound and does not inherit an instrument that has been wrong three times. |

### OBS-BGSTAB-005 — Observe-only TerminalResourcePolicy compiler and consumer inventory

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | observability |
| Target | wave-3 |
| Status | implemented |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | buildergate, wave-3, terminal-resource-policy, observe-only, orca-absorption, github-issue-8 |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | 동일 config corpus의 반복 compile 결과, canonical/legacy/conflict/invalid N-1/N/N+1와 consumer inventory fixture를 unit test하고 observe mode에서 runtime decision이 legacy baseline과 byte-for-byte 동일한지 integration test로 검증한다. |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/BuilderGate/issues/8 |
| Related Docs | docs/research/2026-07-15.orca-terminal-stack-absorption-research-and-implementation-plan.ko.md, docs/plans/2026-07-15.projectmaster.orca-terminal-performance.wave-master.plan.md |

#### Requirement

BuilderGate는 모든 terminal buffer, queue, snapshot, runtime residency와 delivery limit을 typed TerminalResourcePolicy로 설명할 수 있어야 하며, observe-only 단계에서는 기존 legacyPolicy를 유일한 적용 결과로 유지하면서 등록된 후보 policy와의 차이만 관측해야 한다.

#### Rationale

현재 buffer·queue·snapshot·runtime 한도가 여러 config와 frontend default에 중복되어 source와 단위가 불명확하다. 먼저 legacy behavior를 바꾸지 않는 inventory/compiler가 있어야 안전한 통합과 deprecation 판단이 가능하다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Machine-readable consumer manifest는 server config/schema/store, PTY/headless model, WebSocket router/send policy, snapshot/replay/repair, browser runtime residency/hidden output, terminal write/recovery scheduler와 persisted snapshot storage의 각 terminal resource key를 unit, source, schemaVersion, profileVersion, legacy alias, apply boundary와 실제 consumer path로 식별해야 한다.
- [x] AC-2: Server는 기존 effective config에서 legacyPolicy를 결정적으로 컴파일하고 같은 입력에 대해 후보 profile policy를 별도로 계산하되 observe 단계에서는 모든 runtime admission, cap, expiry, drop, reconnect와 recovery 결정을 legacyPolicy로만 수행해야 한다.
- [x] AC-3: Compiler는 resourceLimits.terminal의 canonical source와 명시된 legacy fallback의 precedence를 보존하고 invalid/conflicting input을 다른 값으로 조용히 보정하지 않아야 한다.
- [x] AC-4: 후보 profile의 파생식과 값은 별도 stable policy 계약에 등록된 경우에만 비교 대상으로 사용할 수 있으며 등록되지 않은 profile은 unavailable 진단을 반환해야 한다.
- [x] AC-5: 관측 결과는 consumer, legacy decision, candidate decision, source, unit, policy/schema version과 차이 reason을 bounded telemetry로 남기되 terminal payload나 비밀 값을 포함하지 않아야 한다.
- [x] AC-6: Static inventory contract test는 repository의 terminal resource config key, getter와 consumer call site를 manifest와 양방향 대조하고 미등록 key/consumer, orphan manifest entry, 중복 source와 단위 불일치가 하나라도 있으면 실패해야 한다.
- [x] AC-7: 기존 Settings UI, 저장 형식, product default, terminal runtime authority와 legacy 동작은 이 Requirement만으로 변경되지 않아야 한다.

| VE-6 | test | docs/analysis/2026-09-17.issue8-observe-only-policy/00.decision-table.md | AC-1, AC-6 | Issue #8 premise measurement and closure at baseline bc94bea. AC-6 and its two sibling review-regression tests were RED in this tree while AC-6 was checked: cwd=server/, npx tsx --test src/services/TerminalResourcePolicy.test.ts, exit 1, tests 20 / pass 17 / fail 3; the VE-5 guard exit 1. Four causes were stacked behind a single throw and surfaced one at a time. (A) NOT PRE-EXISTING: commit 1ae3787 (REL-BGSTAB-009) relocated the grace lane from WebSocketProvider#bufferGraceMessage into terminalGraceBuffer#applyGraceBufferedMessage and left the consumer catalog naming the old owner. Measured ancestry: 1ae3787 is not an ancestor of a6c7edc (VE-11 HEAD) or 8f8f934 (this Requirement 2026-09-16 Change Note baseline) and is an ancestor of bc94bea, so a regression introduced after those measurements was sheltering under their pre-existing label. (B,C,D) genuinely pre-existing: the ConfigFileRepository classification accessEvidenceSha256 pin went stale when resourceLimits.telemetry.sampleIntervalMs was retired, exposing its @resource-limits-root.[dynamic] render access as an unregistered call site; the reserved-copy-option-decoy fixture pinned a CRLF marker against an LF SessionManager.ts so it failed on its own setup rather than posing its question; and the compact decision list in TerminalResourcePolicyObservations.ts never gained resourceLimits.terminal.checkpointMaxBytes when REL-BGSTAB-023 registered it. FIXES: four catalog tuples and six mutation fixtures re-pointed at the relocated owner; the fixture insertion made newline- and indentation-agnostic with its own match-count assertion; a reviewed re-pin of the ConfigFileRepository access hash, whose only delta since the pin is sampleIntervalMs retired and checkpointMaxBytes added across the apply and render paths, all persistence-boundary, no new dynamic access; the missing compact decision added; manifest and lineage re-sealed. AC-1 WAS ALSO SUBSTANTIVELY FALSE and its own test did not see it: assertRepositoryPath asserts existsSync only, which is true of the correct state and of the defect, so AC-1 stayed green over four entries naming a symbol that no longer consumes. AC-1 now also asserts each entry evidenceSignature occurs in the file the manifest names, and asserts its own check count first so it cannot pass vacuously over an empty set; restoring the pre-fix consumerPath reddens AC-1 through that new assertion by its own message, so the coverage reaches the specific layer. This textual check does NOT establish symbol ownership or liveness, which remain AC-6 only, and would not have caught the relocation had the catalog pinned the symbol leaf name rather than the expression. TWO GUARD CONTRACTS WERE UNSATISFIABLE rather than strict and were split by what each is evidence of: classification accessEvidenceSha256 was held equal to the immutable historical seal although the pin must move whenever a classified file accesses change, and now requires enumeration in the sealed lineage exactly as resource decisions already did; and one regex asserted the focused pass count over both the live run and the sealed PH-001 artifact, satisfiable only by forging that artifact or freezing two suites, now separated into a live count and the artifact own recorded count. RESULT: focused 58/58 exit 0 over TerminalResourcePolicy, RuntimeConfigStore, TerminalResourcePolicyCanary and CanaryPublicFixture; VE-5 guard exit 0 with all nine claims true and activationEligible false; cwd=server/ npm run build exit 0 with docs/analysis/terminal-fairness-authority clean. Dependent regression over the 19 server suites importing the changed modules, measured with server/dist built on both sides: baseline bc94bea 281 tests / 276 pass / 3 fail / 2 skipped / exit 1 versus 281 / 279 / 0 / 2 / exit 0, the three baseline failures being exactly these test names and the unchanged 281 total showing existing assertions were strengthened rather than cases added. OBSERVE-ONLY PARITY UNCHANGED: --rerun-differential refreshed the GREEN snapshot because the compact decision list went 34 to 35, and byteForByteEqual stays true with disabledSha256 equal to observedSha256 at 2fe5453825d00a0a86ba633e0aceef72419170eb855d3a72dee09a0fa05c5287, byte-identical to the SHA the original PH-001 report recorded, disabledCount 0. NOT MEASURED: tier A0 broad server sweep was deliberately skipped because four of its cases bind TCP 2222 and concurrent lanes may hold it, so this evidence covers 19 files and does not speak for the rest; no browser verification, no UI, stored shape, public runtime payload or product default changed; no process started or stopped and 2001/2002 untouched, a negative claim this bundle cannot reproduce. Three wave3 tool suites remain red and each reproduces identically at bc94bea in a clean detached worktree: canary-admission-evidence (its pinned red-test list omits a frontend test another lane added), admission-source-read-wiring and fair-readmission-closure-v3.trust (both assert absolute Windows paths on a Linux checkout). Status/Stability unchanged. |
| VE-7 | test | docs/analysis/2026-09-17.issue8-observe-only-policy/00.decision-table.md | AC-1, AC-6 | Round-2 corrections after an independent hostile review of the issue #8 changes. Nine findings (3 HIGH, 3 MEDIUM, 3 LOW); all nine were acted on, none auto-rejected. VE-6 stands for the runs it describes; the statements it makes that this row supersedes are named here. (1) A FIFTH CAUSE, missed by the audit and found by the review. Hardening the mutation-battery insertion to an exact hit count while leaving the replacement at available >= replacements concealed that the split-owner-does-not-share-evidence fixture had stopped mutating one of its two owners: it declares replaceCount 2 against limits.serverBufferedHighWaterBytes in WsRouter.ts, which now holds three occurrences, two inside WsRouter.sendTransportMessage and one inside WsRouter.flushTransportQueue, and String.replace takes the first match each iteration so flushTransportQueue was never mutated while the fixture stayed green. The three-occurrence state is pre-existing; the concealment was not, because this work rewrote that exact assertion and chose the predicate that hides it. Now assert.equal(available, replacements) with replaceCount 3. RESIDUAL: mutating every occurrence proves both owners must carry evidence, not that one owner evidence cannot be counted for the other, which is what the fixture name claims; that needs occurrence-targeted mutation the harness cannot express. (2) THE CLASSIFICATION GATE WAS A COMMENT, NOT CODE. The relaxed guard moved the anchor from the immutable historical manifest to the mutable lineage, which the re-seal tool writes, and unlike the decision axis there was no operator acceptance flag - while both the tool and guard comments asserted a re-pin could never be a silent side effect. Since a classification pin is a blanket exemption from the unregistered-call-site scan, re-pinning can hide a real consumer. assertDecisionChangeWasAccepted is now generalized over both axes and --accept-classification-change is required. CONTROL RUN: forging the pin to all zeros makes the re-seal exit 1 with refusing to re-seal: the classification exemption pins changed and the lineage record does not enumerate them, and nothing is written; the forged pin was restored byte-identically. (3) THE PIN CLAIM WAS NARROWED. VE-6 said the re-pin followed review of the file delta, implying that delta explained the hash move. Measured: the new value d5ee2b5e94dd is the tool own derivation over the current tree via its pending sentinel, but staging a root whose ConfigFileRepository.ts is the blob from 5918146 - the commit recording 99ca442c - yields a THIRD value, 633123e7ef05, under a scanner whose code did not change in that range. So the pin movement is NOT fully explained by the file delta and the residual is UNEXPLAINED. What the diff does establish is that the file only resource-access change is sampleIntervalMs retired and checkpointMaxBytes added, all persistence-boundary, no new dynamic access, and that is the review basis on which the re-pin was accepted. Candidate cause, not measured: 5918146 created TerminalResourcePolicyInventory.ts whole by splitting it out, so the pin may have been carried across that split from a scanner version not run here, in which case it has been stale since written - reported as a possible pre-existing defect rather than resolved. (4) PARITY WORDING CORRECTED. VE-6 said OBSERVE-ONLY PARITY UNCHANGED citing 2fe5453825d0. The artifact carries two parity pairs and only that one is unchanged; runtimeProjection moved 840faf01a37a to 36b1ac12f063 under this work --rerun-differential. The property that matters, disabled == observed, holds on BOTH pairs before and after; the projection content moved as the expected consequence of registering one more observation decision. Separately and pre-existing, the PH-001 report records that projection SHA as 7d740ada140e, matching neither value. (5) AC-1 COUNT ASSERTION STRENGTHENED AND ITS DESCRIPTION CORRECTED. It was a module-global counter asserted > 0, which the preceding length assertion already implied and which would survive a change checking one entry of 81; it is now local and asserts equality with manifest.consumers.length. VE-6 said it asserts its own check count first - the assertion follows the loop, and first was wrong. VE-6 also said assertRepositoryPath asserts existsSync only; existsSync is its only condition on the file CONTENT, and it also checks the path is relative, backslash-free and inside the repository. (6) The guard emitted report hardcoded focusedTests 24 while the live assertion had moved to 31, so the run designated final published a figure contradicting its own execution; the field is now parsed from the live output. (7) The re-seal absorbed a stale source hash for WebSocketContext.tsx, 93ad4cf286e9 to 31626bdd2181, though this work does not touch that file; the source COUNT change is a pure addition 35 to 36 but the entries were not all untouched, and that is now enumerated. (8) The insertion helper re-indents inserted lines, which is meaning-preserving for JavaScript except inside template literals; it now refuses those. An earlier draft also required every inserted line to be a complete statement and false-reddened same-owner-dead-code-decoy, whose first line is if (false) { - the relaxation was re-verified against all thirteen insertBefore fixtures. (9) The no-process-stopped caveat, present in VE-6, was missing from the report closing line and is now carried there. RE-VERIFIED AFTER ALL OF THIS: focused TerminalResourcePolicy 20/20 exit 0; guard exit 0 with focusedTests 31 and activationEligible false; cwd=server/ npm run build exit 0 with docs/analysis/terminal-fairness-authority clean; dependent sweep 281 tests 279 pass 0 fail 2 skipped exit 0. ONE LOAD-DEPENDENT FAILURE RECORDED AND NOT CLASSIFIED AS A FLAKE: PERF-BGSTAB-010 gated real-session PTY chain settles within the fixture bound after overflow cleanup, in TerminalResourcePolicyCanary.test.ts which this work does not touch, failed once under concurrent full-sweep on a filesystem-watcher assertion; tally red 1 green 7 including two isolated canary runs and a full sweep rerun immediately after the red, and it did not fail in the baseline sweep. Status/Stability/ACs unchanged. |
| VE-8 | test | docs/analysis/2026-09-17.issue8-observe-only-policy/00.decision-table.md | AC-1, AC-6 | Round-3 corrections after a second independent hostile review (11 findings: 3 HIGH, 4 MEDIUM, 4 LOW); all were acted on and two reviewer claims were corrected by measurement rather than adopted. VE-6 and VE-7 stand for the runs they describe; statements they make that this row supersedes are named here. (1) THE CLASSIFICATION PIN WAS NEVER VALID, AND VE-7 RETRACTION WAS ITSELF UNMEASURED. VE-7 said the pin movement was unexplained and offered a candidate cause. Both were wrong. The scanner code is unchanged from 5918146 to HEAD, so the pin can simply be recomputed against historical blobs via the tool own pending sentinel: 1f76132 gives b988b5e4, 5918146 - the commit that RECORDED 99ca442c - gives 633123e7, ed03769 gives 9c7bd117, and bc94bea gives d5ee2b5e. 99ca442c is none of them, so it was invalid at the commit that wrote it rather than going stale; decision-table cause (B) naming sampleIntervalMs as the staling mechanism is wrong as stated, and the candidate cause is refuted because 5918146^ contains no TerminalResourcePolicyInventory.ts and no such pin. MOST CONSEQUENTIAL: d5ee2b5e was ALREADY correct at bc94bea - necessarily so, since this work never touched ConfigFileRepository.ts - and exactlyClassifiedPaths admits a path only on hash equality, so that file was NEVER exempt from the unregistered-call-site scan and the re-pin GRANTS an exemption it has never held rather than restoring one. The justification is therefore what the exemption covers, measured with the stale pin in place: exactly one residual, @resource-limits-root.[dynamic], being the two resourceLimits[sectionName] element accesses in the JSON5 render helpers, unchanged since wave0 1f76132, in a file classified persistence-boundary that applies no terminal runtime decision. The invalid original pin is a pre-existing defect in 5918146, reported rather than resolved. (2) FIVE manifestDrift FIXTURES SHARED ONE DISJUNCTION AND IT COULD NOT TELL THEM APART. It accepted missing-tuple OR orphan-tuple OR source-hash-mismatch, and since every mutated file is in evidenceSourcePaths the last fires for any byte change at all. The review said all five passed vacuously; MEASURED, three do produce genuine tuple drift (object-option-noop-callee-decoy with three orphan and three missing, call-input-noop-callee-decoy and control-guard-wrong-return-decoy with one each) and only the two unchanged-guard-after-* fixtures produce source errors alone - which matches what PH-001 declared about them, that inserting if (true) return ahead of an unchanged guard is caught by the coarse source seal rather than by the AST. So the reviewer claim was too strong and is corrected rather than adopted. Each fixture now declares driftMechanism and is held to it: tuple fixtures must produce tuple drift, seal-only fixtures must produce a source hash mismatch AND no tuple drift, so a decoy the AST later learns to see reddens and gets promoted instead of staying in the weaker class. (3) THE split-owner RESIDUAL RETRACTED ONE LEVEL TOO FEW. VE-7 said mutating every occurrence shows both owners must carry evidence. Discovery throws on the FIRST catalog entry with a missing signature and the catalog lists sendTransportMessage before flushTransportQueue, so the fixture rejects on the first owner whether or not the second is mutated: it proves the first owner must carry evidence and CANNOT observe the second at all. What replaceCount 3 plus the exact-count assertion buys is a tripwire for the next occurrence-count change, not per-owner discrimination. (4) AC-1 COUNTER WAS A TAUTOLOGY AFTER THE ROUND-2 FIX. The increment moved into the caller loop over manifest.consumers and was compared against that same array length, so gutting assertEvidenceSignaturePresentAt would leave AC-1 green. The helper now returns true and the caller counts completions. (5) The insertion helper still rejoined the WHOLE file with one inferred newline, so a mixed-ending target would have every line ending rewritten, including inside its own template literals; mixed endings are now refused. The blank-marker assertion added in round 2 was unreachable - placed after the hit-count assertion that would fire first - and is moved ahead of the marker construction. (6) The guard pass-count parse was anchored against raw output although the adjacent write path strips CRLF, which would false-red on a Windows checkout; it now parses a normalised copy, fail 0 is anchored for the same reason, and focusedTests carries the test count with focusedPass carrying the pass count, since reporting a pass count under a tests name under-reports by exactly the number of todo or skipped cases. (7) RECORD: the watcher tally is now split by tree (round-2 tree red 1 green 3, earlier trees green 4, baseline green 2) because an earlier draft wrote at this tree over four trees and said seven greens where ten preserved logs hold nine; VE-7 said the relaxation was re-verified against thirteen insertBefore fixtures where grep gives nineteen, and all nineteen were verified to contain no backtick; and the log first named R2-FINAL-dependents is the RED run while the cited final figures come from R2-FINAL-dependents-rerun. RE-VERIFIED: focused TerminalResourcePolicy 20/20 exit 0, guard exit 0 with focusedTests 31, focusedPass 31 and activationEligible false. Status/Stability/ACs unchanged. |

The count assertion is kept as a loop-completion check and its comment says that is all it is. (5) THE WATCHER TALLY IS NO LONGER A NUMBER SOMEONE TYPED. #### Research / Analysis

- Orca는 얕은 socket gate와 bounded queue의 단일 ownership을 보여주지만 BuilderGate의 사용자 설정 profile compiler를 제공하지 않는다. 따라서 Orca 상수를 복사하지 않고 기존 effective config를 authoritative input으로 삼는다.

#### Implementation Notes

- 기존 schema, RuntimeConfigStore와 PublicRuntimeConfig를 재사용해 server-side pure compiler로 구현하고 session generation 시작 시 effective legacy policy를 고정한다. 새 Settings UI는 만들지 않는다.
- [2026-07-26] Evidence correction: final PH-001 immutable manifest and green evidence establish 80 exact consumer tuples; prior 71-tuple completion-note text was an intermediate-count transcription error. Status, Stability, acceptance criteria, and evidence references unchanged.
- [2026-07-26] Evidence clarification: the final 80-tuple count derives from the sealed historical manifest, PH-001 report, and top-level green-evidence seal. The raw green evidence's nested role/source-set summary is stale (71-role total), preserved unchanged, and reconciled by ph-001/green-evidence-correction.json; this note changes no Status, acceptance criterion, or Verification Evidence link.
- [2026-07-26] Verification-count correction: the prior 23/23 completion-note count was a preliminary transcription. The final PH-001 report and preserved raw focused green evidence establish 24/24; this clarification changes no Status, acceptance criterion, Verification Evidence reference, or historical raw artifact.

### REL-BGSTAB-010 — Non-loss TerminalResourcePolicy enforcement canary

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | reliability |
| Target | wave-3 |
| Status | implemented |
| Priority | high |
| Tags | buildergate, wave-3, terminal-resource-policy, nonloss-canary, rollback, github-issue-9 |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | evolving |
| Verification Method | cap 증가/감소, 기존 entry 유무, capability N-1/N/N+1, invalid profile, mid-drain rollback과 다중 client 격리 fixture를 TDD하고 legacy baseline의 bytes/order/expiry/generation hash 불변을 검증한다. |
| GitHub Issue | https://github.com/Snoworca/BuilderGate/issues/9 |
| Related Docs | docs/research/2026-07-15.orca-terminal-stack-absorption-research-and-implementation-plan.ko.md, docs/plans/2026-07-15.projectmaster.orca-terminal-performance.wave-master.plan.md |

#### Requirement

TerminalResourcePolicy enforcement는 observe evidence와 stable policy contract를 통과한 선택 대상에서만 canary로 활성화되어야 하며, 기존 entry와 recovery state를 손실·재해석하지 않는 전이만 적용하고 실패 시 대상별 legacyPolicy로 되돌릴 수 있어야 한다.

#### Rationale

여러 buffer 설정을 한 번에 바꾸면 이미 적재된 데이터의 의미와 recovery generation이 바뀔 수 있다. 기존 entry를 보존하는 좁은 전이만 허용해야 성능 실험이 correctness를 훼손하지 않는다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Canary는 capability가 확인되고 대상 policy contract가 최소 stable이며 명시적으로 선택된 client/session에만 적용되어야 하고 그 밖의 대상은 legacyPolicy를 계속 사용해야 한다.
- [x] AC-2: Cap 증가는 새 effective limit으로 적용할 수 있지만 cap 감소나 admission 제한은 기존 queued/retained entry의 bytes, expiry, generation, ordering, ready와 recovery 상태를 변경하지 않고 새 admission 경계부터만 적용해야 한다.
- [x] AC-3: Browser sole writer와 server authority가 승격되기 전에는 canary가 기존 reliable output을 drop하거나 view를 stale로 바꾸거나 reconnect를 강제하거나 recovery direct write를 호출해서는 안 된다.
- [x] AC-4: 각 consumer 전이는 policy/version, 이전·다음 effective decision, 적용 generation, accepted/rejected reason과 rollback 결과를 bounded ledger에 기록해야 한다.
- [x] AC-5: Compiler/consumer mismatch, unsupported profile, invalid transition 또는 canary failure는 해당 대상의 legacyPolicy로 명시적으로 복귀하고 다른 client/session, PTY producer와 authority state를 변경하지 않아야 한다.
- [x] AC-6: Canary 중지와 rollback은 새 admission을 먼저 legacyPolicy로 되돌리고 기존 entry가 원래 lifetime과 ordering으로 drain된 뒤 ledger를 종료해야 한다.
- [x] AC-7: TerminalResourcePolicy consumer registry는 신규 queue consumer 등록 계약을 명시해야 하며, 정적 contract test가 registry에 등록된 모든 consumer id가 최소 하나의 consumer catalog 항목에서 사용되는지 검사해야 한다. 사용처가 없는 id는 사유와 결정 주체가 기록된 명시적 reservation 으로만 허용되고, 그 reservation 은 manifest consumer tuple 을 가지지 않아야 하며, 등록 누락·근거 없는 reservation·tuple 을 가진 reservation 중 하나라도 있으면 실패해야 한다.

MEASURED DEFECT: OBS-BGSTAB-005 AC-6 holds only the direction 'every consumer the AST finds must be registered'. Nothing held the converse, and it had already rotted - server.config.schema is one of the 11 TERMINAL_RESOURCE_POLICY_CONSUMER_IDS with zero of the 81 CONSUMER_CATALOG entries and zero of the 81 tuples in the sealed current manifest (terminal-resource-consumer-manifest.current.json, which is the file this suite reads; the older terminal-resource-consumer-manifest.json holds 80 and an earlier draft of this row cited that number against the current manifest, which was wrong), and neither the type system nor the scanner can see that. Its disposition is NOT decided here: docs/issues/wave4-wave5/20-consumer-rollout-tracker.md already records the 11-versus-10 gap and assigns 'missing registration or inapplicable' to wave4-wave5 step 0, so the reservation names that owner and reason and the open decision now fails loudly at the guard instead of resting on someone reading that document. STRICT TDD: test authored first, red on the missing export; validator implemented, red with unused-consumer-id server.config.schema which is the real defect rather than scaffolding; reservation recorded, green. RESULT cwd=server/ npx tsx --test src/services/TerminalResourcePolicyConsumerRegistry.test.ts exit 0 tests 6 pass 6 fail 0 skipped 0 todo 0; npx tsc --noEmit exit 0. MUTATION COVERAGE AS MEASURED AT THE TIME OF THIS ROW, SUPERSEDED BY VE-8 (10), which re-ran the battery against the reordered function at ten mutations, discounted a contaminated column and found one genuine survivor that this row's figures do not reflect: five mutations, each with an asserted substitution count and a verified applied edit, all five caught, four by the test named for them - deleting the unused-consumer-id push reddens 'rejects a registered consumer id that nothing uses'; the reservation-has-tuple push reddens 'refuses a reservation for an id that still owns manifest tuples'; the reservation-without-reason branch reddens 'rejects a reservation with no reason or no decision owner'; the reservation-not-registered branch reddens 'rejects a reservation for an id outside the registry'; stopping the checked increment reddens the two rows that assert it. The battery was run again unchanged after the suite changed. ALLOWLIST DISCIPLINE: reservation membership is pinned by deepEqual over consumer ids rather than by a count, so a rename or a paired swap costs an argued edit; a reservation needs a non-empty reason AND a named owner; and a reserved id must own no manifest tuple. RETRACTED AFTER REVIEW: that last condition was described here and in the code as an independent check that a genuinely consuming id cannot be parked in the reservation list. It is not. discoverTerminalResourceInventory pushes exactly one tuple per CONSUMER_CATALOG entry, so on a fresh seal the manifest id set is identical to the catalog id set - measured at 81 entries and 81 tuples over the same 10 distinct ids - and the tuple branch therefore cannot fire for an id that is not already used. It is a tripwire against a stale or hand-edited manifest, nothing more. WHAT REMAINS UNKNOWN, stated rather than estimated: this guard cannot distinguish a registered and genuinely unused id from one that consumes through a call site nobody catalogued, because the manifest is derived from the catalog and the two look identical to it; separating them needs a signal the current inventory design does not produce, since a consumer id exists only in the catalog. FIXTURE PROVENANCE, CORRECTED: the production row first derived manifestConsumerIds from catalogConsumerIds, which made the two agree syntactically; the manifest ids now come from the sealed current.json and the catalog ids from the TypeScript source. That change removed only the syntactic derivation. The two still agree BY CONSTRUCTION, because the manifest is generated one tuple per catalog entry, and a later round added a freshness assertion that actively pins the seal to a hash of the file holding the catalog - so the two fixtures are now provenance-coupled by an assertion rather than merely equal by accident. The consequence, recorded rather than implied: reservation-has-tuple is unreachable on the production path, since it needs an id that is not used yet owns a tuple, and the freshness assertion rejects the stale manifest that is the only state where those differ. Its fixture encodes exactly that rejected state, so the branch is defence in depth behind a separate contract, not a live discriminator. TSCONFIG MEMBERSHIP PROVEN BY CONTROL, not by a green run: injecting a deliberate TS2322 into the new file makes tsc exit 2 naming that file, restored byte-identically. WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE: the guard is reachable by someone who types the command, not enforced - nothing invokes it at build, lint, hook or CI time, and this repository runs no tests in CI. WHAT THE METHOD CANNOT SEE: one-at-a-time statement deletion cannot produce a reordering of the reservation checks, nor an argument swapped between catalogConsumerIds and manifestConsumerIds at a call site. NOT CHANGED: no enforcement promoted, no stable contract registered, no Settings UI, stored shape, public runtime payload or product default touched; no process started or stopped and TCP 2001/2002 untouched, a negative claim this row cannot itself reproduce. |

| VE-7 | test | tools/wave3/canary-admission-evidence.test.mjs | AC-1, AC-2, AC-3, AC-4, AC-5, AC-6, AC-7 | Round-1 corrections after an independent hostile review of the issue #9 changes at commit edb2bef. Ten findings (2 CRITICAL, 3 HIGH, 4 MEDIUM, 1 LOW); ALL TEN were acted on and none was auto-dismissed - the --auto policy would have auto-rejected the LOW, and the standing rule against auto-dismissing record-accuracy findings was applied to the whole set instead. Earlier rows stand for the runs they describe. BOTH CRITICALS WERE VERIFIED BY MY OWN DERIVATION BEFORE BEING ACCEPTED, and both were right. (1) CRITICAL, THE SEAL HAD STOPPED OBSERVING THE RUN. Removing the volatile totals from the sealed records went one step too far: every field of sealedProjection was a constant. failed/cancelled/skipped/todo are forced to zero by the all-pass assertion that throws earlier in summarizeFocused, and registeredResultSha256 hashed sorted(registeredNames), which are literals declared in the guard itself and never touched by the run. This is the equal-and-opposite error to the one being corrected: having found that the seal recorded too much volatile data, the fix left it recording no run data at all. registeredResultSha256 was changed to hash the observed TAP status of each registered name read out of the executedTests map. CORRECTED IN A LATER ROUND AND FALSE AS WRITTEN: a reviewer reproduced both sealed values offline from the guard own literal name lists with every status forced to pass, matching the committed bytes exactly, because THREE assertions force that status before the hash is reached - the exit-status assertion in runCommand, the all-pass assertion in summarizeFocused, and its per-registered-name assertion. The field is the old one with pass appended and adds no falsification power except against the simultaneous weakening of all three. See VE-8; the seal is now described as what it is rather than dressed up a third time. The comment no longer overstates it: the live assertions force these values today, and the artifact evidence that is falsifiable today is inputHashes, productionSourceHashes and productionRuntimeRegistry stdoutSha256. (2) CRITICAL AND A RETRACTION, THE SECOND CONDITION WAS NOT INDEPENDENT. VE-5 and the code claimed a reserved id owning no manifest tuple was a second condition a copied name could not satisfy. MEASURED: discoverTerminalResourceInventory pushes exactly one tuple per CONSUMER_CATALOG entry, so on a fresh seal the manifest id multiset equals the catalog id multiset - 81 entries and 81 tuples over the same 10 distinct ids, verified by re-deriving both from the committed bytes - and the tuple branch therefore cannot fire for an id that is not already used. It discriminates only on a stale or hand-edited manifest. The claim is retracted in VE-5, in the module comment and in the suite header rather than softened, and no replacement check was invented at the time, on the ground that the reviewer own suggested fix was not constructible either. CORRECTED IN A LATER ROUND AND FALSE AS WRITTEN: that was an overclaim in the opposite direction, made in the sentence immediately after a retraction, which is exactly where understatement feels like rigour. A later reviewer constructed two checks from the committed bytes and one is implemented as reservation-id-occurs-in-production. See VE-8 (5). What remains unknown is stated rather than estimated. (3) HIGH, THE RESERVATION LIST WAS A ONE-KEYSTROKE ALLOWLIST. Two new error codes close the cheapest routes: duplicate-reservation, since the Map construction silently kept the last of a duplicated id, and reservation-for-used-id, since the used-id continue meant a reservation for an id already in the catalog was never examined. The suite also now asserts the sealed manifest is FRESH by comparing evidence.sourceHashes for TerminalResourcePolicyInventory.ts against that file on disk, so a stale seal can no longer widen the hole silently; that assertion caught its own trigger immediately, reddening on the fixer own edit until the re-seal. (4) HIGH, A FACTUAL ERROR IN AN IMMUTABLE ROW: VE-5 said 80 sealed manifest tuples; the file the suite actually reads holds 81, and the 80 belongs to the older manifest. Corrected in VE-5 with both numbers named. (5) HIGH, THE EXECUTED HALF OF THE NEW DECLARATION AXIS WAS AN UNAUDITED LABEL. Nothing compared executedRequirementBearingTestSourcePaths against focusedCommands, so listing a new suite as executed and never running it was strictly cheaper than the declared route, which at least costs a written reason. Every executed path is now asserted to appear in the args of its lane command, with an audited count. CORRECTED IN A LATER ROUND: that count was asserted AFTER the loop and was itself the tautology this same work removed elsewhere - an unconditional increment inside a loop compared against the length of the array it iterates, which over an empty array passes as 0 equals 0. It is replaced by a non-empty assertion placed before the loop. See VE-8. The flat tokenising described in this row was itself replaced in a later round by a positional operand check of equal strength on both platforms, and later still the per-lane operand constant it compared against was deleted as a transcription of the command it audits. (6) MEDIUM, checked WAS A RESTATEMENT OF THE INPUT: the increment was the first statement of the loop, so it equalled consumerIds.length for every execution and said nothing about whether the predicate ran. It now counts ids that reached the used-or-reserved decision, and the suite asserts the exact value with a message naming the new meaning. (7) MEDIUM, THE SUITE RE-CREATED THE ROT IT WAS REMOVING: assert.equal(consumerIds.length, 11) pinned a live registry count as a bare literal in the same commit whose argument is that exactTests 42 and 133 rotted because the cheapest repair was retyping a number nobody owns. Registering a twelfth consumer would have reddened on the number before anything meaningful. Replaced with a uniqueness assertion and a non-empty check; the informative failure is already the unused-consumer-id error. (8) MEDIUM, VE-3 and VE-4 were left standing for AC-1..AC-6 with figures this work proves false for this branch, so a reader had to reach the third row to learn the first two were wrong; both are now annotated inline as superseded by VE-6 for this branch. (9) MEDIUM, COLLATERAL SURFACE THE EARLIER ROW DID NOT SCOPE. Other sealed records also hash TerminalResourcePolicyInventory.ts. MEASURED: docs/analysis/kiwi-coder-2026-07-24.pm.fair-admission/provenance-manifest.json pins working_sha256 5a14b19d with git_status ?? , which matches neither the parent commit e001790 value 9c4ab754 nor any value in this work, so that pin was ALREADY STALE BEFORE THIS BRANCH TOUCHED THE FILE and this work cannot have broken it; and docs/analysis/kiwi-coder-2026-07-26.pm.fair-readmission/final-protected-input-manifest.json declares observation_only true with the note dirty frozen worktree observation; not canonical SRS verification. NOT RUN: the captureFrozenProvenance closure suites, because a single call costs about 89 seconds and the closure gate is separately recorded as red at baseline; this is a scoped exclusion, not a clean result. (10) LOW was fixed rather than waved through, as part of (3). The failure later reproduced in a direct invocation with no guard involved, and a control isolated the real condition as concurrent execution of the four-file focused command - the identical files with --test-concurrency=1 are green, and the canary file alone is green. | VE-8 | test | server/src/services/TerminalResourcePolicyConsumerRegistry.test.ts | AC-1, AC-2, AC-3, AC-4, AC-5, AC-6, AC-7 | Round-2 and round-3 corrections after a second independent hostile review of the issue #9 changes at commit 257b2e2. Twelve findings (1 CRITICAL, 3 HIGH, 5 MEDIUM, 3 LOW); all twelve acted on, none auto-dismissed. Earlier rows stand for the runs they describe, and the sentences of VE-7 and VE-5 that this round proved false were corrected IN PLACE. An earlier draft of this sentence said two sentences of VE-7; a later reviewer found a third, the claim that no replacement check was constructible, and it is annotated too. The count is recorded as corrected rather than silently fixed, because every miscount in this lane has run low rather than left for a reader to discover three rows later, which is the disposition VE-7 itself applied to VE-3 and VE-4. (1) CRITICAL, THE ROUND-1 FIX WAS ITSELF VACUOUS AND THE REVIEWER PROVED IT RATHER THAN ARGUED IT. The reviewer recomputed both sealed registeredResultSha256 values offline, from the guard own literal name lists with every status forced to pass, and matched the committed bytes exactly. Three assertions force that status before the hash is reached: the exit-status assertion in runCommand, the all-pass assertion in summarizeFocused, and its per-registered-name assertion. The new field was the old field with pass appended. NO THIRD VERSION WAS ENGINEERED. The comment now names all three assertions, says the value is a constant on any run reaching the seal, and says plainly that this block is NOT evidence a run occurred and that the artifact falsifiable run-derived evidence is inputHashes, productionSourceHashes and productionRuntimeRegistry stdoutSha256. THE UNDERLYING LESSON, recorded because it was got wrong twice in opposite directions: a seal written only on a green run cannot record anything but green. Round 1 sealed too much volatile sibling data; round 2 sealed a dressed-up constant and called it an observation. (2) THE SAME TAUTOLOGY WAS REINTRODUCED IN THE COMMIT THAT REMOVED IT. The audited-count assertion added to close the executed-label finding was an unconditional increment inside a loop compared against the length of the array it iterates, asserted AFTER the loop, which over an empty array passes as 0 equals 0 - the identical shape as the checked counter fixed twenty lines away in the same commit, and VE-7 described it as being asserted before the property when it was after. Replaced by a non-empty assertion placed before the loop, counter deleted. (3) AND A BARE LITERAL OVER A LIVE QUANTITY WAS REINTRODUCED TWO DOZEN LINES AFTER ONE WAS DELETED ON PRINCIPLE: assert.equal(result.checked, 1) pinned the count of registered ids absent from the catalog, which moves the moment anyone registers or catalogues a consumer. Replaced by the derived form with a non-empty guard. THREE INSTANCES OF ONE PATTERN inside work whose central argument is about that pattern; the count is recorded rather than tidied. (4) THE RETRACTION SURVIVED VERBATIM AT ITS POINT OF USE. The sentence calling the manifest-tuple branch a second independent condition was retracted in the module comment, the suite header, VE-5 and VE-7, but left standing in the body of the test it describes, four lines from the fixture. Corrected there, and VE-5 now also records that reservation-has-tuple is unreachable on the production path and that its fixture encodes a state the freshness assertion rejects earlier. (5) THE RETRACTION OVERSTATED IN THE OTHER DIRECTION. Saying no replacement check is constructible was a new unevidenced claim, and the reviewer constructed two from the committed bytes. One is now implemented: reservedIdProductionOccurrences plus the error code reservation-id-occurs-in-production, which fires when a reserved id literal appears in a production source outside the declaration sites that must name it. MEASURED AT THE TIME OF THIS ROW: server.config.schema occurred 3 times across the sources then scanned, once in TerminalResourcePolicy.ts as the registry entry and twice in TerminalResourcePolicyInventory.ts, the reservation entry and one mention inside the comment arguing it - the second inventory occurrence was found by the implementer against a brief that said one. SUPERSEDED BY VE-9 AND VE-10: widening the scan raised the count to 4, the per-file table was replaced first by a path-plus-id-plus-kind table and then by line-and-window anchors with exact per-entry occurrence counts, and the claim that a third mention reddens was false of the per-file form - a reviewer demonstrated that deleting the comment mention and adding a real consuming call in the same file kept the count and hid the consumer with no edit at all. Residual after exclusion is 0. WHAT THIS STILL DOES NOT COVER, stated rather than estimated: a consumer that consumes without ever naming the id, reached through a variable, a re-export or an alias. That residual is genuinely unreachable from a literal scan; the earlier wording claimed the whole question was, which was wrong. (6) FRESHNESS WIDENED FROM ONE ENTRY TO ALL OF THEM: the assertion compared a single recorded source hash and ignored the aggregate beside it, so the seal could be stale against the rest of the tree while reporting fresh. All 36 recorded entries are now compared, with the entry count asserted before the loop. (7) THE win32 MEMBERSHIP CHECK WAS WEAKER THAN THE OTHER PLATFORM: flat-tokenising the joined command string let a path satisfy the audit from a flag value or an excluded position. It is now positional on both platforms - operands after the --test token, no flag-shaped tokens, compared as a set against an explicit per-lane operand list - and both lanes are asserted to have been checked. (8) THE DUPLICATE-RESERVATION DEFERRAL is documented as diagnostic loss rather than a correctness escape. (9) THE COLLATERAL ENUMERATION WAS INCOMPLETE AND SCOPED OUT SOMETHING INSPECTION ANSWERS. VE-7 said other sealed records also hash this file and then measured two; at least ten name it. The four provenance manifests under pm.fair-readmission and pm.fair-readmission-current pin a hash over import-specifier edges rather than file content, and this work changes no import or require line in that file, so they are provably unaffected by derivation rather than by assumption; ph-001/green-evidence.json stores the path string only. The captureFrozenProvenance closure suites remain NOT RUN, and that stays a scoped exclusion rather than a clean result. (10) MUTATION BATTERY RE-RUN AGAINST THE REORDERED FUNCTION, closing the blind spot VE-5 names. Ten mutations, each with an asserted substitution count and a verified applied edit, including TWO REORDERINGS, which one-at-a-time deletion cannot produce. CONTAMINATION FOUND AND DISCOUNTED RATHER THAN REPORTED AS COVERAGE: the production row fails under EVERY mutation, because mutating the module changes its sha256 and the new freshness assertion detects it, so that column carries no information about any mutation. Discounting it, nine of ten were caught by the test named for them and ONE SURVIVED - disabling the duplicate-reservation continue left the suite green, so the documented deferral was an unverified defensive statement. The raw failure count would have read ten of ten. A fixture pinning the deferral was added, and M10 was RE-MEASURED as caught by the test named for it; the implementer reasoned the kill and the reasoning was not accepted as the measurement.
